# Match-First Revamp Traceability

Date: 2026-05-23

Scope: Match-first revamp phase traceability, including Phase 1/2 closure
evidence, Phase 3 backend 100% closure repair, Phase 4 progress/success UI,
Phase 5 Netherlands results map/list, Phase 6 selected-neighborhood detail
completion, Phase 7 house-to-Dossier bridge evidence, and Phase 8 final
QA/accessibility/analytics/failure-state closure. Phase 7 includes
provider-backed nearby address selection through the backend PDOK Locatieserver
reverse path plus localized manual recovery; Phase 8 final evidence is recorded
in `docs/qa/final_evidence.md`.

## Selected-Neighborhood Building Eager Paging Cap 2026-05-23

Status: **PASS FOR QUICK FRONTEND PERFORMANCE REPAIR; FINAL NEAR-INSTANT
BUILDING ARCHITECTURE STILL NEEDS CACHING/PREWARMING OR VECTOR TILES**.

| Requirement / change | Exact work completed | Files changed | Verification |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| PRD Phase 7 and Acceptance 12; selected-neighborhood detail must progressively show scoped building footprints with honest partial states, without making users wait on long provider cursor chains. | Added a red-first regression test proving eager selected-neighborhood building paging stops after 3 backend page requests, renders the loaded 3 pages, leaves `data-building-complete="false"`, and shows existing partial-loading copy when more pages remain. | `frontend/src/test/match-first-neighborhood-detail.test.tsx` | Red-first `cd frontend && npx vitest run src/test/match-first-neighborhood-detail.test.tsx -t "stops eager"` failed before implementation because the component fetched a fourth page and rendered `data-rendered-buildings="4"`. The same focused command passed after implementation with 1 test. |
| Prevent dense neighborhoods such as Hof van Delft from running the old 50-request eager page loop on the user-visible path. | Reduced `MAX_BUILDING_PAGE_REQUESTS` from 50 to 3 in the selected-neighborhood detail loader. Existing merge, cursor, selected-bounds, partial-state, and loaded-footprint behavior remains intact. | `frontend/src/components/match-first/NeighborhoodDetail.tsx` | `cd frontend && npx vitest run src/test/match-first-neighborhood-detail.test.tsx` passed with 53 tests. `cd frontend && npx eslint src/components/match-first/NeighborhoodDetail.tsx src/test/match-first-neighborhood-detail.test.tsx` passed. `cd frontend && npm run build` passed with existing placeholder assetlinks/AASA production-release notices. Existing React `act(...)` warning noise remains in one selected-neighborhood map-state test. |

Residual release risk:

- This quick repair makes the detail view stop quickly and label partial
  coverage, but it does not make all dense-neighborhood footprints complete
  near-instantly. The production-grade follow-up is backend cache/prewarming or
  selected-neighborhood vector tiles so live PDOK BAG pagination is not on the
  user's critical path.

## Selected-Neighborhood Building Page Rate-Limit Fallback Repair 2026-05-23

Status: **PASS FOR SCOPED FRONTEND REPAIR; EAGER BUILDING PAGE LOADING STILL
NEEDS A FUTURE PACING/VIEWPORT PASS**.

| Requirement / change | Exact work completed | Files changed | Verification |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| PRD Phase 7 and Acceptance 12; selected-neighborhood detail must keep available scoped building footprints visible and use honest partial/missing states. | Reproduced Hof van Delft behavior: PDOK BAG building pages were valid, but a later cursor request hit backend `429 Rate limit exceeded: 20 per 1 minute`; before the repair the frontend discarded the already loaded footprints and showed "Building data is unavailable right now." Added a regression test for one successful page followed by a rate-limited cursor request. | `frontend/src/test/match-first-neighborhood-detail.test.tsx` | Red-first `cd frontend && npx vitest run src/test/match-first-neighborhood-detail.test.tsx -t "rate limited"` failed before implementation with `data-rendered-buildings="0"` and `data-fallback-reason="building_layer_failed"`. The same command passed after implementation with 1 test. |
| Preserve selected-neighborhood-only 2D BAG footprint rendering while preventing later cursor failures from blanking earlier pages. | Updated the building page loop so first-page failures still show the unavailable failure state, but failures after at least one successful page keep the merged buildings in state, set `complete: false`, preserve partial copy, and avoid the missing-footprint fallback. | `frontend/src/components/match-first/NeighborhoodDetail.tsx` | `cd frontend && npx vitest run src/test/match-first-neighborhood-detail.test.tsx` passed with 52 tests. `cd frontend && npx eslint src/components/match-first/NeighborhoodDetail.tsx src/test/match-first-neighborhood-detail.test.tsx` passed. |
| Build and source hygiene for the scoped frontend repair. | No i18n resources changed; existing partial/unavailable copy keys remain in use. No Dossier, scoring, amenity, route, or backend provider contracts were changed. | same component/test files plus this traceability file and handoff | `cd frontend && npm run build` passed with existing placeholder assetlinks/AASA production-release notices. `git diff --check -- frontend/src/components/match-first/NeighborhoodDetail.tsx frontend/src/test/match-first-neighborhood-detail.test.tsx` passed with CRLF normalization warnings only. |

Residual release risk:

- Dense neighborhoods can still have more building pages than should be eagerly
  loaded in one burst. This repair keeps already loaded footprints visible and
  labels the state as partial, but a future pass should pace cursor loading or
  make it viewport-driven to avoid hitting backend rate limits.
- Existing React `act(...)` warning noise remains in one selected-neighborhood
  map-state test.

'-'" src/components/match/MatchAdminDashboard.tsx src/components/match/MatchAdminDashboard.test.tsx src/i18n/en.json src/i18n/nl.json` found the localized fallback key/resources and did not find the old `report_id ?? '-'` fallback. `cd frontend && npm run build`, `cd frontend && npm run test:a11y`, `cd frontend && npm run test:perf`, and `git diff --check -- frontend/src/components/match/MatchAdminDashboard.tsx frontend/src/components/match/MatchAdminDashboard.test.tsx frontend/src/i18n/en.json frontend/src/i18n/nl.json` passed; diff check emitted CRLF warnings only. |

  audit is still not complete.

## Progressive Selected-Neighborhood Building Footprint Implementation 2026-05-22

Status: **PASS FOR PROGRESSIVE SELECTED-NEIGHBORHOOD FOOTPRINT PAGING AND
PARTIAL-STATE UX**.

| Requirement / change | Exact work completed | Files changed | Verification |

| PRD AC12 / Phase 7 / Phase 6A: selected-neighborhood detail should show every available building footprint inside the selected neighborhood or selected-neighborhood viewport, loaded progressively when needed, and must not silently present a representative sample as complete. | Added backend building response completion metadata (`complete`, `next_cursor`, `loaded_scope`, `partial_reason_code`) and a cursor-aware `/buildings` query. ## Amenity Empty-Filter Visibility Repair 2026-05-20

| Requirement / bug | Exact work completed | Files changed | Verification |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| User reported that amenities were not visible on the selected-neighborhood map. Root cause found in the frontend filter path: a relevant amenity tag could be activated even when the backend returned zero marker points for that amenity category, causing the map to hide other returned amenity markers and appear empty. PRD Phase 7 / AC13-AC14 requires selected-neighborhood amenity overlays and a right-side relevant-amenities legend/filter panel without invented POIs. | Added marker-count-aware amenity filtering. Tags with zero returned marker points are disabled as filters, stale active filters are cleared when refreshed amenity data no longer includes points for that category, and a localized EN/NL no-marker state appears when tags exist but no official marker points are available yet. This preserves the contract that the map renders every returned backend amenity point while avoiding blank maps from empty filter selections. | `frontend/src/components/match-first/AmenityTags.tsx`, `frontend/src/components/match-first/NeighborhoodDetail.tsx`, `frontend/src/components/match-first/NeighborhoodDetail.css`, `frontend/src/i18n/en.json`, `frontend/src/i18n/nl.json`, `frontend/src/test/match-first-neighborhood-detail.test.tsx`, `docs/ai/latest_handoff.md`, `docs/qa/match_first_revamp_traceability.md` | Red-first: `cd frontend && npm run test -- src/test/match-first-neighborhood-detail.test.tsx -- -t "keeps map markers visible"` failed before implementation because the Parks / green space filter was enabled with no returned Parks marker points. Final: `cd frontend && npm run test -- src/test/match-first-neighborhood-detail.test.tsx -- -t "keeps map markers visible\|toggles amenity filters\|official street basemap"` passed; `cd frontend && npm run test -- src/test/match-first-neighborhood-detail.test.tsx` passed with 37 tests and existing React `act()` warning noise; `cd frontend && npm run test -- src/test/match-i18n.test.ts src/test/match-first-copy-guard.test.ts` passed with 9 tests; `cd frontend && npx eslint src/components/match-first/NeighborhoodDetail.tsx src/components/match-first/AmenityTags.tsx src/test/match-first-neighborhood-detail.test.tsx` passed; `cd frontend && npm run build` passed with existing placeholder assetlinks/AASA notices. ## Neighborhood Detail House Hit And Zoom Repair 2026-05-19

| Requirement / bug | Exact work completed | Files changed | Verification |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Houses on `nh_seed_missing_data_example` were still perceived as tiny and in the middle of a field. Diagnosis showed the selected-detail CRS path now uses canonical RD New: the seed viewport is `[119200,459200,120800,460800]`, WGS84 `[4.8643178,52.1198621,4.887847,52.1343436]`, and 3DBAG returns 49 LoD 2.2 buildings inside that rural fixture. The remaining product bug was interaction/framing: empty or central basemap clicks could select the first building, a selected house did not zoom to its footprint, and tiny true-scale projected meshes could collapse to a few pixels at neighborhood zoom. PRD Phase 6 / AC12-AC14 require scoped houses to be visible, inspectable, and selectable without misleading field clicks. | Added Leaflet-container footprint hit-testing so only real footprint or near-footprint clicks select a house; removed arbitrary first-building fallbacks for empty and central basemap clicks. Added selected-house `fitBounds()` framing with selected padding and `maxZoom: 19`. Added minimum projected-size expansion for tiny LoD 2.2 roof geometry so scoped houses stay inspectable. ## Show My Matches Latency Repair 2026-05-19

| Requirement / bug | Exact work completed | Files changed | Verification |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Clicking the review CTA `Show my matches` delivered results far too slowly. PRD Phase 4 / AC8-AC9 require a friendly backend-backed progress state and verified success/results without making the match job itself artificially slow. | Diagnosed the path end to end. The deterministic match computation was under a second, but backend `run_match_job()` wrote every transient progress stage and each `match_job_running` event as separate local-SQLite transactions, making the in-process run path about 12.8s before status/results reads. Fast deterministic jobs now persist bounded lifecycle state: queued remains pollable, one running lifecycle event is recorded only when needed, result/job/session-success persistence shares one transaction, and status/results reads use one database connection in the normal completed path. The frontend progress screen now polls immediately after a queued `POST /run` response instead of waiting for the queued poll interval. Slow/failed/fallback/no-strong-match public states, result identity gating, deterministic weighted scoring, and analytics coverage are preserved. The local backend server on `127.0.0.1:8000` was restarted after the API change. | `backend/app/services/match/jobs.py`, `backend/app/services/match/instrumentation.py`, `backend/tests/test_match_jobs.py`, `frontend/src/components/match-first/MatchingProgressScreen.tsx`, `frontend/src/test/match-first-progress.test.tsx`, `docs/ai/latest_handoff.md`, `docs/qa/match_first_revamp_traceability.md` | Red-first backend: `cd backend && pytest -q tests/test_match_jobs.py -k "seed_match_records_one_running_lifecycle_event"` failed because a normal seed match persisted 7 `match_job_running` events. Red-first frontend: `cd frontend && npm run test -- src/test/match-first-progress.test.tsx -- -t "polls queued runs immediately"` failed because no status request was made until `poll_after_ms` elapsed. Final: `cd backend && pytest -q tests/test_match_jobs.py tests/test_match_instrumentation.py tests/test_match_sessions.py` passed with 34 tests; `cd backend && pytest -q tests/test_match_jobs.py tests/test_match_results_contract.py tests/test_match_neighborhood_layers.py` passed with 57 tests; `cd backend && python -m ruff check app/services/match/jobs.py app/services/match/instrumentation.py tests/test_match_jobs.py tests/test_match_results_contract.py tests/test_match_neighborhood_layers.py` passed; `cd frontend && npm run test -- src/test/match-first-progress.test.tsx src/test/match-i18n.test.ts src/test/match-first-copy-guard.test.ts` passed with 33 tests; `cd frontend && npx eslint src/components/match-first/MatchingProgressScreen.tsx src/test/match-first-progress.test.tsx` passed; `cd frontend && npm run build` passed with existing placeholder assetlinks/AASA notices and existing `vendor-three` chunk warning. Timing evidence: comparable in-process API sequence dropped from about 16.8s before to about 6.4s after; live local API after backend restart returned `POST /run` in about 1.7s and completed the measured session-to-results sequence in about 7.1s. Residual risk: local SQLite latency still dominates final answer sync/run start; next optimization is returning preference vector readback from `PATCH /answers` to skip the extra `GET /sessions/{session_id}`. |

## Survey CTA Responsiveness Fix 2026-05-19

| Bug | Exact work completed | Files changed | Verification |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Survey confirmation could feel non-responsive or very slow because selecting an answer started backend answer persistence and the CTA then awaited another `/answers` PATCH before moving forward. This affected intermediate Next transitions, the final Question 11 `Review answers` CTA, and then exposed a review-screen race where `Show my matches` could read incomplete backend state before the final background save finished. The live app also froze because frontend fire-and-forget analytics and critical match-session writes reached the Turso backend concurrently; the session request returned HTTP 500 after about 11 seconds. | Changed survey CTA behavior to validate and locally persist the current answer, advance/open review immediately, and let backend answer persistence complete in the background. The review screen's `Show my matches` transition now actively PATCHes the displayed review answers before backend vector readback and run creation, preserving the PRD rule that matching cannot start from unconfirmed backend state. Stale-session recovery now routes recovered sessions to the latest visible question if the user advanced while recovery was pending. Turso DB contexts are now serialized in `get_db()` so analytics writes cannot overlap and break session, answer, or run writes in the current backend process. | `backend/app/db.py`, `backend/tests/test_db.py`, `frontend/src/components/match-first/SurveyShell.tsx`, `frontend/src/components/match-first/SurveyShell.test.tsx`, `frontend/src/App.tsx`, `frontend/src/App.test.tsx`, `docs/ai/latest_handoff.md`, `docs/qa/match_first_revamp_traceability.md` | Red-first focused test failed before implementation because an unresolved answer PATCH kept the heading on Question 1. A second red-first recovery test failed before the follow-up because a late recovered session was not routed with the advanced step. A final survey follow-up failed because `Review answers` did not call `onReview` while the final PATCH was unresolved. A review-run follow-up failed because `Show my matches` read an incomplete backend session without first PATCHing the displayed review answers. A backend red-first test failed because two Turso DB contexts overlapped. Final: `cd frontend && npm run test -- src/App.test.tsx -- -t "syncs displayed review answers"` passed; `cd frontend && npm run test -- src/App.test.tsx -- -t "routes the final survey CTA|syncs displayed review answers|requires backend vector|blocks review completion|restores a direct review route"` passed with 5 selected tests; `cd frontend && npm run test -- src/test/match-first-survey.test.tsx src/components/match-first/SurveyReview.test.tsx src/components/match-first/SurveyShell.test.tsx` passed with 30 tests; `cd frontend && npm run test -- src/test/match-i18n.test.ts src/test/match-first-copy-guard.test.ts` passed with 9 tests; `cd frontend && npm run build` passed; `cd backend && ruff check app/db.py tests/test_db.py` passed; `cd backend && pytest -q tests/test_db.py tests/test_match_sessions.py` passed with 14 tests. | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Numbered recommendation circles on the PDOK results map could appear in the wrong location or drift while the map moved. The root cause was a split projection model: the real basemap and polygon overlays were Leaflet layers, while the visible numbered circles were React buttons absolutely positioned from a static WGS84 bounding box. A follow-up browser symptom showed a single partial PDOK tile and blank map area because Leaflet could initialize from a stale or zero-sized mobile panel and no later size invalidation occurred. | Removed the fixed CSS percentage marker overlay and rendered the numbered recommendation circles as Leaflet `DivIcon` markers at each recommendation centroid. Leaflet now projects and repositions the numbered markers during pan and zoom. Added scheduled `invalidateSize` calls after map creation and basemap attachment, plus `ResizeObserver` handling while the map panel is active, so PDOK tiles recalculate to fill the visible panel. The localized marker labels, selected state, map/list synchronization, analytics events, and list fallback are preserved. The stale `aria-hidden` on the Leaflet container was removed so the projected marker buttons remain reachable. | `frontend/src/components/match-first/ResultsMap.tsx`, `frontend/src/components/match-first/ResultsMap.css`, `frontend/src/test/match-first-results-map.test.tsx`, `docs/ai/latest_handoff.md`, `docs/qa/match_first_revamp_traceability.md` | Red-first focused marker regression failed before implementation because `Show IJburg on map` was not inside `.leaflet-marker-pane`. Red-first size regression failed before implementation because `L.Map.prototype.invalidateSize` was never called and the test DOM showed a `0x0` Leaflet overlay. Final: `cd frontend && npm run test -- src/test/match-first-results-map.test.tsx -- -t "renders numbered recommendation markers"` passed; `cd frontend && npm run test -- src/test/match-first-results-map.test.tsx -- -t "invalidates the Leaflet size"` passed; `cd frontend && npm run test -- src/test/match-first-results-map.test.tsx` passed with 14 tests; `cd frontend && npm run test -- src/test/match-i18n.test.ts src/test/match-first-copy-guard.test.ts` passed with 9 tests; `cd frontend && npm run build` passed; `cd frontend && npm run test:a11y` passed with 9 tests and existing unrelated React `act(...)` warnings; direct Chromium Playwright `npx playwright test --project=chromium tests/e2e/match-first-final-journey.spec.ts -g "complete match-first journey" --workers=1 --reporter=line --timeout=90000` passed with 1 test. The npm wrapper form timed out after 244 seconds before reporting results, so the direct Playwright command is the focused browser evidence. Residual risk: marker accuracy now depends on upstream recommendation WGS84 centroids being correct. |

## Survey Session Recovery Fix 2026-05-19

| Bug | Exact work completed | Files changed | Verification |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| A mobile survey tab could keep a stale match session ID after local backend/dev-server restarts. Selecting a valid later survey answer, such as Q10 "Village", then showed the generic save-failure alert and blocked progress even though the answer itself was valid. | Added a recoverable `match.session.not_found` path in `SurveyShell`: create a fresh backend session through `App`, copy the local survey answers into the recovered session snapshot, retry the answer PATCH, and route subsequent survey navigation to the recovered session ID. Generic network failures still stay on the current question with the localized save-failure alert. | `frontend/src/App.tsx`, `frontend/src/components/match-first/SurveyShell.tsx`, `frontend/src/components/match-first/SurveyShell.test.tsx`, `docs/ai/latest_handoff.md`, `docs/qa/match_first_revamp_traceability.md` | Red-first focused test initially failed because no recovery callback was called and the generic alert stayed visible. Final: `cd frontend && npm run test -- src/components/match-first/SurveyShell.test.tsx` passed with 15 tests. Manual mobile Playwright stale-session check on `http://127.0.0.1:5173/` verified the 404 -> fresh session -> retry 200 -> Question 11 route sequence. |

The final remediation closed backend intent/budget pruning, backend over-limit multi-select persistence, direct survey-question route skipping, stale vector readback, sessionless legacy survey routes, backend save failure handling, richer review context, derived question counts, full keyboard-completion proof, backend session hydration, per-question focus management, backend warning-code surfacing, and frontend/backend survey-contract parity gaps. Later phases still need real async matching completion, results hydration, selected-neighborhood 3D map/detail hydration, and production run-status/result contracts.

Non-review and stale-vector requests return stable errors without creating jobs. | `cd backend && pytest -q tests/test_match_jobs.py` |
| Phase 3 API contract; T-031 | `backend/app/api/match.py`, `backend/tests/test_match_jobs.py`, `specs/002-match-first-revamp/contracts/match-first-api.md` | Session/run/status/results endpoints return contract stable codes such as `match.session.not_found`, `match.job.not_found`, `match.results.not_ready`, and `match.results.not_found`, and all Phase 3 session/run/status/results responses include `Cache-Control: no-store`. | `cd backend && pytest -q tests/test_match_jobs.py` |
| FR-M2; PRD Sections 14.4-14.5 | `backend/app/services/match/jobs.py`, `backend/app/db.py`, `backend/tests/test_match_jobs.py`, `backend/tests/test_match_db_schema.py` | Match jobs are persisted, pollable, idempotent for retry of the same vector, and separate lifecycle `status` from progress `stage`. In-progress jobs expose `status="running"` with the current stage/message key; concurrent duplicate run requests against the same active queued/running vector reuse the same `job_id`, keep one `active_job_id`, schedule one background execution, and persist one result set. `/run` inserts the queued job before feature-store loading; a later feature-store failure becomes a pollable failed job. Jobs older than 10,000 ms report `matching_slow` once while preserving the same job and current stage. Stale active jobs are recovered through the endpoint before retry; expired jobs can be rerun from the final review CTA without duplicating active jobs. | `cd backend && pytest -q tests/test_match_jobs.py tests/test_match_db_schema.py`; final focused Phase 3 command passed with 50 tests. |

Predictive probability remains unavailable for MVP. | `cd backend && pytest -q tests/test_match_results_contract.py`; final focused Phase 3 command passed with 50 tests; `cd frontend && npm run build`; `cd frontend && npm run test -- src/test/match-first-model-honesty.test.ts` |

Closure status after 2026-05-16 review repair and audit repair verification: **Phase 4 progress and success states are 100% complete for the documented scope.** The flow now runs final matching through the backend, polls status, handles slow/failed/expired/cancelled/fallback/no-strong-match public states, validates terminal result payload identity before success including present/exact `result_set_id`, shows a distinct localized results-unavailable recovery when result hydration fails or terminal result identity is missing, shows the reduced-motion-aware branded checkmark only after verified results, and transitions by CTA to the results route. Phase 6 selected-neighborhood 3D/detail and Phase 7 Dossier bridge were outside this Phase 4 closure and are covered in later sections. Full repo lint was attempted previously and still has pre-existing files outside the Phase 4 touched surface; touched Phase 4 lint targets passed. Phase 4 a11y evidence is component-level axe plus existing keyboard/focus coverage, not a full browser touch-target audit.

## Phase 7 Dossier Bridge

Status: **PASS for Phase 7**. Selected server-side house/building candidates
resolve to existing Dossier routes with match context, and ambiguous server
candidates call the backend PDOK Locatieserver reverse path to return selectable
nearby address choices before Dossier entry. Deterministic first/second seed
candidates, provider-backed ambiguous seed candidates, and selected-candidate
Dossier entry are covered by backend and browser tests. Dossier keeps the
persistent localized Back to match map action; direct address-search Dossier
entry remains unchanged. The latest repair tightened the backend trust
boundary, stale/no-address/manual recovery, analytics timing/privacy, touch
targets, reduced-motion handling, and browser round-trip evidence without
starting Phase 8.

Ambiguous server-side house candidates use backend PDOK Locatieserver reverse lookup for nearby address choices. Candidate selection is validated against the server-side selected-neighborhood candidate set and route context uses server values. Bridge logic lives in the existing scoped building service rather than a new Dossier module. | `cd backend && ruff check app tests/test_match_neighborhood_layers.py` passed. `cd backend && pytest -q tests/test_match_neighborhood_layers.py tests/test_export_entitlement.py tests/test_reports_api.py` passed with 42 tests. `cd backend && pytest -q tests/test_locatieserver.py -k reverse` passed. Bridge tests cover resolved, PDOK-backed candidates, selected candidate to Dossier, manual_required, unavailable, provider-empty/provider-failure manual recovery, stale result-set/metadata rejection, required selected result identity, spoofed candidate field rejection, spoofed return selected-house rejection, spoofed candidate IDs, stable invalid-VBO errors, and no-store responses. |

Status: **LOCAL AUTOMATED GATES PASS; RELEASE RESEARCH/PROFILE ITEMS PARTIAL**.
Phase 8 closes the local automated QA slice for the match-first revamp, while
human usability research, live production/mobile profiling, provider-backed 3D
coverage remain release conditions. Detailed command output and notes are recorded in
`docs/qa/final_evidence.md`; no row below is marked pass without automated or
manual evidence.

| PRD AC1 and SC-001; first-time primary-action understanding | PARTIAL / RELEASE RESEARCH. They do not prove that first-time users understand the action. | `MatchFirstLanding`/App tests, `test:a11y`, and final E2E are local implementation evidence only. Human/product research evidence for AC1/SC-001 remains required before public release; SC-003 mobile completion research also remains blocked. |

The final journey E2E asserts exact once-per-flow counts for `match_landing_cta_clicked`, `match_final_run_cta_clicked`, `match_results_map_opened`, `match_recommendation_selected`, `match_dossier_opened`, and `match_back_to_map_return_success` in local storage and backend analytics POSTs. `match_neighborhood_detail_opened` remains the detail-entry event. | `backend/tests/test_match_first_analytics_api.py`; `frontend/src/services/matchFirstAnalytics.test.ts`; `frontend/src/components/match-first/SurveyShell.test.tsx`; `frontend/tests/e2e/match-first-final-journey.spec.ts`. 2026-05-18 follow-up evidence: backend spec-contract parity parses the active spec contract against the match-first endpoint catalog; backend tests prove legacy events are rejected by `/api/match/analytics`; survey failed PATCH records `match_survey_answer_save_failed` and does not record `match_survey_answer_saved`; exact-count E2E failed before the repair because `match_results_map_opened` fired twice, then passed after the success-button duplicate was removed; the contract-drift regression failed red-first while `match_neighborhood_clicked` remained in catalogs and ResultsMap output, then passed after the "View neighborhood" action emitted `match_recommendation_selected` and detail-open analytics stayed with `match_neighborhood_detail_opened`; the final journey Show-on-map -> View-neighborhood path asserts recommendation selection exactly once locally and in backend POSTs; `ResultsMap` does not re-emit selection when the recommendation is already selected; focused frontend analytics/results-map command passed with 23 tests; final journey E2E passed with 12 tests. |
| PRD Sections 19.1-19.3; Constitution V/XV; analytics privacy | PASS for minimization/filtering. ## Selected-Neighborhood Amenity Marker Repair 2026-05-20

Status: **PASS FOR SCOPED AUTOMATED REPAIR**.

Acceptance-linked evidence:

- PRD selected-neighborhood amenity overlays now have both sides covered:
  frontend renders every returned official point with type-specific marker
  shapes and marker-count-aware legend filters, and backend can now supply
  official selected-bounds geometry markers for parks/green space and sports
  fields even when the stored amenity table is empty for the selected
  neighborhood.
- The backend fallback remains scoped to the selected neighborhood RD bounds,
  uses official PDOK/BAG geometry providers only, is configurable/time-limited,
  and keeps empty/error responses out of the response cache.
- Slow official geometry providers are bounded at the service layer, so a
  provider delay degrades to available amenity tags/no live points instead of
  the visible "Amenity tags are unavailable right now" state.
- The frontend keeps a localized no-marker state for true empty categories and
  disables zero-point filters so a relevant tag cannot blank the map.
- The selected-neighborhood frontend retries transient amenity tag failures and
  replaces the visible unavailable state when the retry succeeds, covering the
  stale open-page case after a backend/provider hiccup.

Verification:

- Red-first frontend proof:
  `cd frontend && npm run test -- src/test/match-first-neighborhood-detail.test.tsx -- -t "keeps map markers visible"`
  failed before the filter repair.
- Red-first backend proof:
  `cd backend && pytest -q tests/test_match_neighborhood_layers.py -k "selected_geometry_markers"`
  failed before the on-demand geometry fallback because selected-neighborhood
  amenity points were empty when the store had no records.
- Final backend commands passed:
  `cd backend && pytest -q tests/test_match_neighborhood_layers.py -k "slow_selected_geometry_lookup or selected_geometry_markers or amenities_are_preference_aware or amenity_cache_key"`;
  `cd backend && pytest -q tests/test_match_neighborhood_layers.py -k "amenities_are_preference_aware or selected_geometry_markers or amenity_cache_key"`;
  `cd backend && pytest -q tests/test_match_neighborhood_layers.py`;
  `cd backend && pytest -q tests/test_match_amenity_ingestion.py tests/test_match_neighborhood_layers.py`;
  `cd backend && ruff check app/config.py app/models/match.py app/services/match/amenities.py tests/test_match_neighborhood_layers.py`.
- A local live fallback probe against `nh_rotterdam_katendrecht` returned 790
  official `parks_green` PDOK geometry points before the service-level point
  limit is applied.
- A local live endpoint probe for the first generated result returned `200`
  with amenity tags and 80 marker points in about 7.3 seconds after the bounded
  fallback change.
- Final frontend commands passed:
  `cd frontend && npm run test -- src/test/match-first-neighborhood-detail.test.tsx -- -t "retries transient amenity|keeps selected map and 2D fallback usable when amenity tags fail"`;
  `cd frontend && npm run test -- src/test/match-first-neighborhood-detail.test.tsx`;
  `cd frontend && npm run test -- src/test/match-i18n.test.ts src/test/match-first-copy-guard.test.ts`;
  `cd frontend && npx eslint src/components/match-first/NeighborhoodDetail.tsx src/components/match-first/AmenityTags.tsx src/test/match-first-neighborhood-detail.test.tsx`;
  `cd frontend && npm run build`.
- Local service validation: the backend Uvicorn process on `127.0.0.1:8000`
  was restarted because the existing process predated the repair and had no
  `--reload`; the Vite preview on `127.0.0.1:4174` was restarted after the
  rebuilt frontend bundle was generated.

Residual risks:

- This 2026-05-20 residual risk is superseded by the 2026-05-21 school and
  childcare repair above: those sources now have a bounded scoped on-demand
  fallback, while absent official records still return unavailable metadata.
- If PDOK/BAG geometry providers time out or return no selected-bounds records,
  the app shows the honest no-marker state rather than fabricated amenities.

## Selected-Neighborhood Boundary Enforcement 2026-05-22

Status: **PASS FOR SCOPED AUTOMATED REPAIR; FRONTEND BUILD BLOCKED BY UNRELATED
APP WIP**.

Acceptance-linked evidence:

- PRD selected-neighborhood boundary rule is now enforced for the map-layer,
  buildings, and amenities path: official CBS boundary geometry is required for
  selected-neighborhood overlays, and the backend no longer emits a seed display
  bbox as the selected boundary.
- IJburg stale seed metadata is handled by accepting current CBS wijk/buurt name
  variants. A local live probe resolved `nh_amsterdam_ijburg` to
  `cbs_wijk_en_buurtkaart_2024 / wijken / WK0363MJ / IJburg-West`.
- When the official boundary is unavailable, `/map-layers` returns an explicit
  unavailable empty `MultiPolygon`, building and amenity layers are marked
  unavailable with `matchFirst.neighborhood.boundaryUnavailable`, and building
  provider calls are skipped.
- Building and amenity responses are clipped to official boundary geometry.
  The frontend skips building fetches when the layer is unavailable and shows
  localized unavailable-boundary copy rather than drawing a rectangle.
- Grey building polygons remain PRD-compliant deferred BAG `pand` footprints:
  not selectable as house candidates because linked VBO semantics do not show
  `woonfunctie`, because the pand has zero linked VBOs, or because it is
  non-residential/`overige gebruiksfunctie` only.

Verification:

- Red-first backend proof:
  `cd backend && pytest -q tests/test_match_neighborhood_layers.py -k "current_wijk_name_variant or seed_bbox_as_selected_boundary or provider_without_official_boundary" --tb=short`
  failed before implementation and passed after the repair.
- Red-first frontend proof:
  `cd frontend && npx vitest run src/test/match-first-neighborhood-detail.test.tsx -t "does not draw a display-bounds rectangle"`
  failed before implementation and passed after the repair.
- Final backend selected-neighborhood suite:
  `cd backend && pytest -q tests/test_match_neighborhood_layers.py --tb=short`
  passed with 52 tests.
- Adjacent backend provider/amenity suite:
  `cd backend && pytest -q tests/test_bag_ogc.py tests/test_match_amenity_ingestion.py --tb=short`
  passed with 11 tests.
- Backend lint:
  `cd backend && ruff check app/services/match/geometry.py app/services/match/buildings.py app/services/match/amenities.py tests/test_match_neighborhood_layers.py`
  passed.
- Frontend focused tests:
  `cd frontend && npx vitest run src/test/match-first-neighborhood-detail.test.tsx src/test/match-i18n.test.ts`
  passed with 50 tests.
- Frontend lint:
  `cd frontend && npx eslint src/components/match-first/NeighborhoodBuildingLayer.tsx src/components/match-first/NeighborhoodDetail.tsx src/test/match-first-neighborhood-detail.test.tsx`
  passed.
- Frontend build:
  `cd frontend && npm run build` is currently blocked by unrelated unused
  declarations in `frontend/src/App.tsx` for the in-progress
  additional-preferences flow.

Residual release risk:

- If official CBS boundary lookup is unavailable, selected-neighborhood overlays
  pause rather than showing boundary-shaped building/amenity data. This is the
  intended honest fallback but leaves the detail map without overlays until the
  boundary source recovers.
- The full frontend build still needs the unrelated `App.tsx` unused
  additional-preferences declarations resolved.

Status: **PASS FOR LOCAL CI-EQUIVALENT GATES**.

Acceptance-linked evidence refresh:

- Direct unverified results routes now have defensive result-shape validation in
  `ResultsMap`, preserving the neutral "Results unavailable" recovery required
  for stale/unverified match state.
- Failed-progress retry coverage now follows the live post-retry poll state
  instead of assuming the initial queued copy remains visible.
- The lazy `vendor-three` chunk budget reflects the current selected-neighborhood
  3D implementation while continuing to assert that Three is not modulepreloaded
  into the initial document.

Residual release risk:

  payload, but a future performance pass should split or reduce the 3D chunk.

## Punch-List Fixes 2026-05-13 to 2026-05-14

- Match-return Dossier URLs without `lookup`, such as `#/address/{vbo_id}?match_return=...`, now keep the user in the Dossier route and render a localized address-unavailable shell instead of redirecting to Search.
- The Back to match map action is rendered by the Dossier route wrapper whenever match-return context exists, so it remains available through loading, empty, error, and successful address states.
- Match run `completed` / `running` state is no longer trusted from `localStorage`. Until backend run/status/results endpoints are wired, direct success and results routes render neutral localized shells rather than implying that matching has completed.
- Survey answers are scoped by match session ID, preventing a new session from inheriting stale answers from an earlier one.
- `App.tsx` reuses the shared route parser for structured `match_context`, preserving selected neighborhood, selected house, map center, zoom, and list scroll on cold-start Dossier URLs.
- Direct match neighborhood URLs do not trust persisted Dossier return context from `localStorage`; that context is only reused for the active Dossier back action.
- Failure and unavailable copy now has explicit bilingual keys, enforced by `frontend/src/test/match-i18n.test.ts`, for no strong matches, slow backend, failed backend, completed-with-fallback, no reliable address, no-result states, missing 3D, neutral results placeholders, and Dossier address-unavailable recovery.
- Neutral placeholder copy is used for direct unverified success, results, and neighborhood routes until backend-backed completion is verified; explicit progress and failed-backend copy is reserved for confirmed run/status states.
- Dossier match-return context now preserves mobile map/list mode plus selected result ID and rank, reducing context loss when returning from a house Dossier to the match map.
- Settings is hidden during match-first onboarding screens so the flow remains one decision per screen.
- Match route transitions respect reduced-motion preferences.
- Failed match progress states now expose a localized retry action that retries the same match session instead of forcing users to restart the survey.
- Direct Phase 1 survey question routes above step 1 are rejected until the final survey question set lands in Phase 2.
- Back-to-match-map context is rehydrated after refresh only when the user has actually activated the Dossier Back to match map action; passive or arbitrary localStorage context still cannot unlock direct neighborhood placeholders.
- Survey review copy now says Housing goal / Woondoel instead of Search type / Zoektype.
- Direct neighborhood placeholder routes are included in the Phase 1 accessibility coverage.
- Invalid Phase 1 match routes now recover back to the active match survey as the focused primary action, with address search kept as a secondary option.
- Restored selected-neighborhood placeholders now use neutral restored-context copy and expose a Back to survey action while preserving route context attributes for later map/detail hydration.
- App-driven route changes no longer suppress the next real hash route, and browser Back/Forward route changes now re-run the Phase 1 hash-route parser.
- Loaded Dossier exits that send users back to search now preserve match-return context so the existing Back to match map path survives the next address selection.

## Current Phase 1 Limits

- The survey is intentionally one dummy question for Phase 1 shell completion. Final 10-12 question content, backend answer persistence, and preference-vector generation remain Phase 2 work.
- The final survey action now starts backend matching, polls run status, verifies result payload hydration before success, and keeps the visible results map as a Phase 5 placeholder.
- Neighborhood-only 3D building loading is not changed in this phase; no national 3D loading path was introduced.
